## The essays without the reminder of names

**R92. `modules.json` pins `modules` v0.2.12 with the index `sha256:cb16bb45…1d71a`: in window 0.4 it has seven packages, and the current versions are those of v0.2.7 but `novatrail/english-essays` 0.2.1, the essays without the paragraph on names in five task statements. `api`'s code does not change, and the merge makes a release of `api`, since `modules.json` is among the paths `release.yml` builds a release from.**
- **What the release carries:** the index of v0.2.12 is that of v0.2.7 with one more entry, `novatrail/english-essays` 0.2.1, format 0.4, package `sha256:5975e7c5…de9fb5`; the releases v0.2.8–v0.2.11 add no package. The paragraph "Do not write your name or the real name of anyone you know." left five statements, since `app` shows that reminder above Check; `module-kit diff` names it a patch.
- **What was decided:** the pinning as any release of `modules` is pinned, by the digest of its index, with its packages of the window in the image; the tests take each module's current version from the index, so none names 0.2.0 and none changes; the essays come back to the catalog of `novatrail` by `infra`'s list of the deployment's modules after this release (Д47); the change of `canvas.digit_height` waits on `tutor` and is not in it.

— *Why:* the essays return to `novatrail` only as version 0.2.1, with no reminder that `app` already shows.

— *Rejected:* waiting to pin one release with the change of `canvas.digit_height` too — it waits on `tutor`, and the essays would wait with it; an intermediate release, v0.2.8–v0.2.11 — no package of their own.

— *Consequence:* `modules.json`; `internal/learning/release_test.go`; `run/DEBT.md`; `docs/architecture.md`.

## The README of the component

**R93. README.md says what `api` is and where it stands in NovaTrail, and nothing else: its badges, a paragraph on what it does, a list of its links to the platform's other parts, and links to its card in `spec`'s `docs/architecture.md` and to `docs/`. No command, no code block, no instruction, status, version, date, number or language. How `api` is built, checked, released, configured and run — the recipes, the release and its check, the variables, the commands and the jobs of a rollout, the sign-in in production with the calls of Identity Toolkit, the run on the dev projects, with app and for the executor of app — is [build.md](build.md); the list of documents, a short note on releases and the section Contributing are [docs/README.md](README.md).**
- **What is where:** `docs/build.md` — the sections Build and checks, Releases, Configuration, Commands, Sign-in in production and Run on the dev projects, with its With app and For the executor of app; `docs/README.md` — Documents, Releases, Contributing. CLAUDE.md names both; `docs/architecture.md` on the calls of Identity Toolkit, `.bestpractices.json` and the comments of `internal/admission`, `internal/tutorstub` and `scripts/dev.sh` cite `docs/build.md`. No check or test reads a section of README.
- **The release:** the commands of a rollout that the release names are in `docs/build.md` at the release's tag; `infra` runs them by role, not by name, so its deployment does not change.

— *Why:* nobody runs the commands of a README by hand: sessions of Claude Code build, check and run `api`, and read the documents under `docs/` to do it, so README says only what the component is and where it stands; Contributing goes into `docs/README.md` and the details of build and run into a document of their own. The template of `spec` has the same rule for every repository.

— *Rejected:* keeping a short Build and run with the commands in README — a second home of the recipes, which nobody runs from there; dropping what README held — the session reads it, so it moves to `docs/`; spreading it over architecture — architecture says what is built and why, not how to run it.

— *Consequence:* `README.md`; `docs/build.md`; `docs/README.md`; `CLAUDE.md`; `.bestpractices.json`; `internal/admission/admission.go`; `internal/tutorstub/tutorstub.go`; `scripts/dev.sh`; `docs/architecture.md`.

## The cache of the words' instructions

**R94. `api` takes `tutor` v0.1.49, whose Claude adapter caches the instructions of the tutor's words — `CallWords`, `CallHint` and `CallCorrect` — and never the work. The line `ai_usage` of each model carries `cache_write_tokens` and `cache_read_tokens` beside `input_tokens`, which `tutor` counts without the cache; the code of the price and the quota does not change, since `Usage.USD` already counts the cache by its prices.**
- **What the release brings:** the field `provider.Request.CacheInstructions`, set by the requests of the hint and of the words after a correct answer, and a breakpoint of the cache, `cache_control` `ephemeral` of 5 minutes, at the end of the system prompt of such a request in the Claude adapter. The words' model, their prompts, their fingerprints, the admission and the expected prices do not change — `Price` takes the whole input at the input's price, whether a call writes the instructions or reads them. `Usage` carries `CacheWrites` and `CacheReadTokens`, and `InputTokens` without them.
- **The line `ai_usage`:** `cache_write_tokens` is the sum of `Usage.CacheWrites` over the times the cache keeps a write — 5 minutes and an hour, priced apart by `tutor` —, and `cache_read_tokens` is `Usage.CacheReadTokens`; both are written on every line of a model, zero where nothing was cached, as `input_tokens` is. The three add up to the call's input, so the unit economics sum what they did before from the line, now with the share the cache took. They are counts — nothing of the learner's, no work and no words —, so the minimum of data the platform keeps lets them in.
- **The quota:** settlement takes `Usage.USD`, which `tutor` computes from the three by the prices of its table for a read of the cache and a write to it; the reserve stays by `Price`, or by the value of `internal/quota` where `tutor` has none. `Price` takes the whole input at the input's price, so a call that writes the instructions to the cache settles above it by the premium of a write over the input on the instructions, and one that reads them below it: what a request may spend past the cap grows by that premium, beside the output `MaxOutputTokens` bounds.

— *Why:* Opus words the tutor's replies, and the instructions, the same text in every call of their kind, are most of their input; `api` takes the release so that `novatrail` gets the cache. The line without the cache's tokens would show an input smaller than the call's, and the unit economics could not tell a cached call from a short one.

— *Rejected:* one field per time the cache keeps a write — Claude's writes are of 5 minutes, OpenAI's adapter prices writes of 30 minutes and 12 hours, and the price of each is in `cost_micro_usd` already, so the line needs the tokens alone; folding the cache into `input_tokens` — the line would hide what the cache saved, and differ from `tutor`'s `Usage` and its logs of measurement, which count `input_tokens`, `cache_write_tokens` and `cache_read_tokens` apart.

— *Consequence:* `go.mod`; `go.sum`; `internal/logging/`; `internal/rpc/`; `internal/learning/hints.go`; `docs/architecture.md`; `docs/build.md`.

**R95. `api` moves to `spec` v0.18.27 and every call of `.github` to v0.1.33. The submodule and the Go modules `spec` and `spec/contracts` are at the tag; the shared workflows restore the Go caches by the prefix of their key without the hash of `go.sum`. The Go code, `.devcontainer/`, `.claude/`, CLAUDE.md but its tag, RUN.md but its tag, and the layout of the plan do not change.**
- **The template** — the draft of `just new-repo-draft` at the tag, built on a clone of `spec` in a temporary folder, has the README, `docs/README.md` and the line of CLAUDE.md that `api` has; `.claude/settings.json` and the rules of RUN.md are the template's; `.devcontainer/` differs from the draft by `api`'s own differences — nss_wrapper in `toolchain` and the volumes of the dev file and of the custom token — and, in a comment of the Dockerfile on `spec`'s stage `orchestrator`, by "the Dev Containers CLI", which stays: the comment names a stage a copy has not, and a change to the Dockerfile moves the key of the image's cache. `api`'s README passes the README check of `new-repo-check`.
- **`.github` v0.1.33** — every call names its commit `f1c7e9e6f8245fdbdbc23c4091f3c26350e3a44e`, which `gh release verify v0.1.33 -R altnovatrail/.github` accepts.
- **Д43** waits for a run of `main` with warm Go caches after a move of a library that few packages import; a move to a tag of `spec` is none, since most packages import it.

— *Rejected:* the composite action `toolchain` of `.github` v0.1.32 in the job "Modules", for the Go caches of its test — the job takes 122–162 s with a warm image, beside 166–381 s of "Go / test", which holds the critical path of a run (measurements 13), and it would change `modules.sh`, which "Withdrawals" shares in the stage `toolchain-core` that the action does not build.

— *Consequence:* `spec`; `go.mod`; `go.sum`; `.github/workflows/`; `CLAUDE.md`; `RUN.md`; `.bestpractices.json`; `docs/architecture.md`; `run/DEBT.md`.

## The check of a task from paper

**R96. `SubmitWork` checks a task from paper: its first Check derives the key of each task of the frame from `PaperWork.statement`, the work read beside the solution once the statement is read, the attempt keeps the key until it closes, and module-kit judges each task's reading against its own key, an item for each task; a task with no key is "unchecked", and with no key in any task every slot is, with `CAUSE_NO_KEY`. Every task with a key correct closes the attempt "correct" with an empty `solved`, no `next` and no evidence.**
- **The key.** The statement goes to `ReadStatement` of `tutor`, which lists the tasks of the crop, each with its statement as printed and its formula — or, when the attempt keeps the reading "Recognise" made, no call goes and that reading is the list; each formula goes to the solver of module-kit, `paper.Solve`, and the tasks with no formula, or one the solver does not take, go together to one solution, `SolveTask`, whose answer of each `paper.Key` makes its key once it holds to the rules of one. The key is the list, `paperv1.Key.tasks`, each task with its statement, which the reading of the work is given on every Check. A reading of the statement that failed, was not admitted, or met a limit or a refused own key stops the chain, and so does a solution that failed; a crop with no task read is a key of none. The key is `paperv1.Key` in `attempts.paper_key` of the migration `0023`, of an attempt of paper alone and of an open one alone: its trigger `attempts_paper_key_go` erases it as the attempt closes, as `attempt_words_go` erases the words of the hints. A key with no answer is kept too, so a later Check calls no AI and is "unchecked" with `CAUSE_NO_KEY`; calls that derived no key keep none, and the next Check derives it again. Of two first Checks that went side by side the key written first stays: a Check that derived a key is judged by its own, since its work was read against those statements; one whose derivation gave none is judged by the key a Check beside it kept, when its reading went on as many tasks as that key lists, and otherwise its slots are "unchecked" with the outcome of the call that failed.
- **Recognise.** `ReadStatement` of the contract makes the same reading of the statement on the learner's press, by the route of the attempt and the Check's choice of the debug mode, and the attempt keeps it, `paperv1.Reading` in `attempts.paper_reading` of the migration `0028`, of an open attempt of paper alone, erased as it closes by the trigger of `0023`, and in no export. The answer is the statements alone; an attempt that keeps a reading or a key answers from it, `repeated`, with no call. Its reserve is the statement's, and one the quota or the limit refuses is a refusal, since no verdict waits on it; a call that gives no reading is an outcome, nothing kept.
- **The reading and the verdict.** The crop goes to `ReadPaper` of `tutor` with the tasks' statements, one reader of up to three models in turn — Haiku 5.5, Sonnet 5.5, Opus 5.5 —, in any reading mode, its observation with the slot of task n at the place n, 1. Each slot's value is brought to its task's key, `paper.Brought` — "x = 4" to "4", "(C)" to "C" — before `observation.Join` and `Verdicts` on the one item of `paper.Items` of that key; the verdict's `value` is what the reader wrote, and its `crop_box` where the reader saw it. A Check with any task wrong counts one wrong answer, as on a task of a module, and an attempt of paper takes no taps.
- **The quota.** On platform AI the first Check reserves the reading, the statement — unless a reading is kept — and the solution as one charge, which the quota holds whole or not at all, each call settling its part, and a solution the solver makes needless giving its part back, as an essay's assessment does; a later Check reserves the reading alone. Each call reserves `tutor`'s expected price of it, that of its three attempts; the values of the kinds in `internal/quota` are for a price with no expected tokens. A quota that does not hold them leaves the slot "unchecked" with its outcome and keeps no key.
- **The stub** of the dev mode checks by shapes: a statement wider than tall reads into one task, `37+48`, which the solver takes; at least twice as tall as wide into three, `37 + 48 =`, `40 + 45 =` and `50 + 36 =`, whose work reads 85 for each, so two correct and one not yet; otherwise taller than wide is one task with no formula, and the solution gives the same 85; square has no one answer, so the task has no key; the work reads by the shape of its crop, which takes in the tracing paper: more than twice as wide as tall the key, wider but not twice a wrong value, square or taller illegible.
- **A repeat** under the key gives the answer kept: an attempt of paper has no `next`.
- **The hold.** `SubmitWork` holds an attempt of paper by `holdPaper`, beside `hold`, which still refuses one for the other requests.

— *Why:* a formula written on the page goes to the solver, a word problem to AI, and AI's key is one solution, of up to three models in turn, until a paid run of the eval of `task_solving` measures how often it errs; `CAUSE_NO_KEY` is a solution with no one answer a program can compare. The key is derived at the first Check, not at `BeginPaperAttempts`: a page of 20 tasks the learner may never check would cost 20 chains, and the statement comes with the work anyway. The work is read once the statement is, beside the solution: the reading is given the tasks' statements, so that each answer is tied to its task by what the task says rather than by a guess at their order — a frame round several tasks is the case the learner meets most —, and the first Check waits for the statement and then the longer of the two. The key lives on the attempt, which no other request reads, and goes with its close, so no key of a task outlives its attempt. One charge for the three calls keeps the quota from running out between the key and the reading.

— *Rejected:* the key derived at `BeginPaperAttempts`, for the cost above; a frame of several tasks with no key and no verdict, `CAUSE_SEVERAL_TASKS`, which would leave unchecked the case the learner meets most; the work read beside the statement, its answers tied to the tasks by their order; the key kept after the close, for a later attempt of the same page — a new page is a new photo, and nothing ties its tasks to the old; a reserve of each call apart, which could take the key and refuse the reading; a key of no answer forgotten, which would ask AI again on every Check of a task that has none.

— *Consequence:* `internal/learning/paperwork.go`, `recognise.go`, `attempt.go`, `learning.go`; `internal/admission/admission.go`; `internal/quota/quota.go`; `internal/store/attempts.go`; `internal/store/migrations/0023_paper_key.sql`, `0028_paper_reading.sql`; `internal/tutorstub/tutorstub.go`; `docs/architecture.md`.

**R97. The debug mode of the models of a task from paper is open to an account with its right, `debug_models` of its row, on a local run and on the deployment: `DebugService`, in which the account chooses the models of the four calls of a Check of a task from paper and a second reader of the work, sees the tutor's words of a task from paper as actions of their own, and takes what each call returned and cost. `api` serves it on every run whose `tutor` has its own adapters with the key of Anthropic; migration 0027 gives the right to every account there is then, `api debug-access` gives it and takes it by the sign-in UID, and on a local run `API_DEBUG_MODELS=1` gives it to every account.**
- **The right.** `accounts.debug_models`, `false` by default: migration 0027 sets it for every account not marked "deleting" when it runs — on each local database, and on the deployment's as the release that holds it rolls out —, and an account made after it has it off; the deletion clears it with the account's other fields. The checks read it with the account's row they read on every request, into `rpc.Caller.DebugModels`, so that a right given or taken holds from the account's next request, with no query of its own.
- **The command.** `api debug-access grant <uid>` and `api debug-access revoke <uid>` give the right to the account of a sign-in UID and take it (`store.SetDebugModels`), and print `granted` or `revoked`; a UID with no account, or one of an account being deleted, exits 1, and what is no action or no UID is a usage refusal, 2. It reads `API_LOG_LEVEL` and `API_DATABASE_URL`, as the other commands of the operator do, logs a failure with the moment `debug_access`, and names the UID neither in its output nor in its log. How it runs on the deployment is `infra`'s.
- **Where it is served.** `admission.Load` makes `tutor` with `Config.Debug` on every run with its own adapters and the key of Anthropic, `Report.Debug`, and `serve` then makes the store and registers `DebugService`; on the stub, or with no key, each method answers `unimplemented`, and `app` shows no button. Each method checks the right, `Store.Open`: without it, `permission_denied` with no `Refusal`. `FindTasks`, `SubmitWork`, `ReadStatement`, `BeginPaperAttempts` and the tutor's words of a task from paper take the account's choice past the list, and begin an action, only while the account has the right: a choice kept from before the right was taken chooses nothing. The mode works in every profile of the account, a child's too, past admission.
- **The flag.** `API_DEBUG_MODELS=1` gives the right to every account of the run, `debug.New(every)`. `config.AdmissionFrom` refuses it with any value but `1`, with `K_SERVICE` or `CLOUD_RUN_JOB` set, as the stub of the dev mode is, beside `API_TUTOR_STUB`, which calls no model, and without `API_ANTHROPIC_KEY`, since the mode offers the models of Claude alone. The lines `startup` and `admission` say `debug_models`, the flag, `Report.DebugEvery`. `scripts/dev.sh` sets it in `run-ai` and in `app` on the models, and unsets it on the stub and in `dev-check`.
- **The store**, `internal/debug`, is each account's choice and its last 100 actions, in the memory of the process alone. `FindTasks`, `SubmitWork` of an attempt of paper and `ReadStatement` — as a Check, `DEBUG_ACTION_KIND_CHECK`, since the contract has no kind of its own for it — each begin an action by the choice of that moment, give `tutor` the route with `Route.Debug`, and keep the action once its request ends, when it made a call. A first model chosen takes `FindTasks` and `BeginPaperAttempts` past the gate's own refusal, as it takes the calls past the list in `tutor`; `TakeDebugCalls` gives the actions and forgets them, and `GetDebug` the catalog of Claude of `tutor` and the models the gate admits for each call for an adult on platform AI, in the order of their attempts (`Gate.Admitted`). A first model chosen is one attempt, with no later ones. Each call is `tutor`'s record (`tutor.DebugCall`): the model's JSON as it returned it, its tokens, its time, its retries, its cost as `cost_micro_usd` of the log, a failure as the cause an outcome gives, and its place among the attempts of its call, `attempt`; the solver's call, which `api` records itself, holds the key as the JSON of `paperv1.Answer`. An instance that stops forgets the choice, and the calls go as admitted until it is set again.
- **Two readers.** With a second model `tutor` gives two observations of the work, and `joinedPaper` joins them as the pair is, each brought to the key first; the action says whether they differ.
- **The quota.** The reserve is `tutor`'s price on the route with the mode, by the models chosen, and each call settles its usage, as any call of platform AI does.

— *Why:* the author sees and changes which models check a task from paper, what each returned and what it cost, without editing code, on a local run and on the deployment, for the users the author names. The right lives in the account's row, so that giving it needs no release and no rollout, and the migration gives it at once to every account of today, each the author's; the checks read the row already, so the right costs no query. The flag stays for a local run, whose accounts the dev scripts make anew. The log of the calls is never written to the log of `api`, which carries no work and no readings, nor to its database: the memory of the process and of the tab is enough for a run by hand. A service of its own keeps the messages for people as they are.

— *Rejected:* the fields of the debug mode in `FindTasksResponse` and `SubmitWorkResponse`, which would carry them to every build; the calls in the log of `api`, which carries no work and no readings; the choice and the calls in the database — another migration, for a choice that a set again restores, and calls that would outlive the run; a mode beside the stub, whose calls go to no model; the mode on a local run alone, by the flag a deployment refuses — the author wants it on the deployment; a list of sign-in UIDs in the deployment's configuration — a commit to `infra` and a rollout for each user, and today's users written out by hand; a claim of the sign-in service — another tool and its key; the account found by its invite code, as `api tariff` finds it — the operator names a user by the UID the sign-in service shows.

— *Consequence:* `internal/debug/`; `internal/store/migrations/0027_debug_models.sql`; `internal/store/accounts.go`, `deletion.go`; `internal/rpc/checks.go`, `rpc.go`, `methods.go`; `internal/config/admission.go`; `internal/admission/admission.go`; `internal/learning/learning.go`, `paper.go`, `paperwork.go`; `internal/logging/events.go`; `cmd/api/serve.go`, `check.go`, `debugaccess.go`, `main.go`; `scripts/dev.sh`; `docs/build.md`; `docs/architecture.md`.

**R98. The three attempts of a call of a task from paper in `api`: `FindTasks`, `ReadStatement` and `SubmitWork` run for `API_PAPER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT`, 90 s by default, every other method for `API_REQUEST_TIMEOUT`, 30 s, under `API_HTTP_WRITE_TIMEOUT`, 120 s by default; each call reserves `tutor`'s expected price of its three attempts, with no change to the quota; the debug mode lists the models of a call in the order of their attempts and records each attempt's place.**
- **The deadline.** `config.LoadFrom` reads `API_PAPER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT` and refuses one that is not positive or not less than `API_HTTP_WRITE_TIMEOUT`, as `API_REQUEST_TIMEOUT`. The interceptor `limited` gives these procedures, `paperMethods` in `internal/rpc`, their limit and every other its own, the client's `Connect-Timeout-Ms` taken if shorter; `learning` keeps the record "going" of an idempotency key of each for as long (`WithPaperRequestTimeout`), and `sweep` waits the longer of the two before it finishes a mark.
- **The reserve** is `Tutor.Price` of the call through the gate, as for any call with an expected price: `tutor` gives the expected tokens of each of the three models of a call of a task from paper and sums them, and each attempt settles its own usage. A call that the first model did is settled at its price, and the rest of the reserve goes back.
- **The debug mode.** `Gate.Admitted` gives every model of a call's entry, and `GetDebug` a `DebugAdmitted` for each, in order; `record` carries `tutor.DebugCall.Attempt` into `DebugCall.attempt`, and the solver's call is attempt 1.

— *Why:* three attempts of 1–8 s each, as a run of the debug mode by hand showed them, with the work and the statement read side by side and the solution after the statement, fit 90 s with room, while 30 s would cut the third short; the other methods keep 30 s, so a request stuck elsewhere does not hold its handler longer. A reserve of the expected price of three is a few cents on the cap of $0.50 a day, and each attempt settles only what it spent.

— *Rejected:* a reserve of the worst case at the bounds of the three models, about $1.5 for a Check that derives its key, three times the cap of a profile's day; a reserve of each attempt apart, which could take the first attempt and refuse the next; 60 s for the two, too short for a slow third attempt after two that failed; 90 s for every method, which would hold a stuck request three times as long.

— *Consequence:* `internal/config/config.go`; `internal/rpc/rpc.go`; `internal/learning/learning.go`, `paper.go`, `attempt.go`; `internal/admission/admission.go`; `internal/debug/debug.go`, `service.go`; `internal/quota/quota.go`; `cmd/api/serve.go`, `sweep.go`; `docs/build.md`; `docs/architecture.md`.

**R99. `FindTasks` takes the learner's request about the page: the contract's bounds are checked before any call, the request and the frames on the page go to `tutor.TaskFinding` and no further than the call, and the stub answers a request with the left column of the page.**
- **The check.** `requestTaken` in `internal/learning/paper.go`, after the photo's: the request valid UTF-8 of at most 200 code points, no control character and not blank; `found` and `drawn` only with it, at most 20 and 40, each box a rectangle in thousandths of the photo, `0 ≤ min < max ≤ 1000`. A breach is `invalid_argument` with no `Refusal`, naming the field, as for the photo.
- **The call.** The request and the boxes go to `tutor.TaskFinding` as they came; `tutor` turns them into its prompt and drops a frame found over one drawn. The quota, its reserve and the deadline are the finding's, and the record of the key holds the outcome alone, as before.
- **The data.** The request lives in the memory of the request alone: `find` clears it with the photo once the call is done, and neither the log nor the record of the key nor the debug mode holds it.
- **The stub.** With a request, a photo not square has twelve frames one under another in the left quarter, less any that covers a frame drawn by `tutor.CoversDrawn`, so that `app` walks the path without a model.

— *Why:* the bounds are the contract's, and a server that checks them before the reserve spends nothing on a request it would refuse; the request is the learner's words and may carry a name, so it goes no further than the photo does.

— *Rejected:* keeping the request in the record of the key, so that a repeat could show it — a repeat is "repeated" without frames, and the words would outlive the call; a request with no frames, which would leave the model to guess what the learner already drew; a stub that reads the request's words, which would test the stub instead of the path.

— *Consequence:* `internal/learning/paper.go`; `internal/tutorstub/tutorstub.go`; `docs/architecture.md`.

**R100. The tutor's words of a task from paper: the profile keeps its hint mode, "show where" by default, which `SetHintMode` sets as a learning request and `UpdateProfile` as an adult's change; `GetOpeningWords` gives the opening question before the first wrong answer, kept as rung 0; `GetRungWords` and `ExplainDifferently` word the rung of the first task of the frame that is "not yet", and in the mode "show where" the rung's words come with the wavy line under the first mistake. Each call goes through `tutor.PaperWords`, its cascade of three models, and its words through `tutor.PaperBreach`, else `tutor.PaperFallback`'s text.**
- **The mode.** Migration `0024_hint_mode.sql` adds `profiles.hint_mode`, `show_where` or `questions_only`, `show_where` by default, so a profile made before it shows where; `account` maps it to `HintMode`, a `HINT_MODE_UNSPECIFIED` in the settings keeps it, and `SetHintMode` refuses one with `invalid_argument`, and a child's profile with `CAUSE_ADULT_NEEDED`: its mode is the adult's, on the profile's page, as the mockup's `home-switch` shows it. `rpc/methods.go` gives `SetHintMode` and `GetOpeningWords` the right `can_learn`.
- **The opening question.** `GetHint` of an attempt of paper with no wrong answer gives empty techniques and records nothing; `GetOpeningWords` takes the crop of the task as printed, checked as a Check's statement, holds the attempt as `holdAny` does, and is one action across its parts (`rpc.Across`): a reserve of `CallPaperHint`, the call with every task of the frame as `store.PaperKey` has it — none while no Check derived a key —, and the model's words written to `attempt_rungs` at rung 0, which the check `attempt_rungs_opening` keeps to the model's words with no "explain differently". A later request and a repeat give the words kept, `repeated`, with no call; a fallback text is given and not kept, so the next request calls again. After a wrong answer it is `not_current`, on a task of a module `invalid_argument`.
- **The rung.** `GetRungWords` with the crop of the work and `ExplainDifferently` without it find the rung's task in the answer the attempt keeps of the submission that gave the rung: the first slot `not_yet` of the first such submission. The request names every task of the frame with its key and its value read, the task's number, the rung, the language of the profile's interface — English with none —, the style and the questions asked; `CallPaperWords` with the work and `CallPaperHint` without it, their reserve that of the words of a module (`quota.reserves`). The words and the box are kept as a module's are, and `markOf` gives `MARK_KIND_WAVY_LINE` at the box in the mode "show where", the model's words alone, and no mark otherwise.
- **The checks.** `tutor.PaperBreach` with the keys of the frame, and of the opening question with the model's own answers as well, which the answer carries for that alone and `api` keeps nowhere; a breach is `tutor.PaperFallback`'s text of the rung in the profile's language, as a module's fallback text is.
- **The report.** `TUTOR_REPLY_OPENING` is the reply "opening" of `problem_reports` (`problem_reports_reply_check`), with no rung.
- **The debug mode.** Each request of words is an action `WORDS` with its cascade's calls, `DEBUG_CALL_KIND_TUTOR_WORDS` for both kinds, and `GetDebug` lists the models of `CallPaperWords` as the cascade of words; a first model chosen takes the words past the gate as it takes a Check's calls.
- **The stub** words the opening question and a rung from a list of its own, with a box at the band of the rung's task — the task's share of the crop's height —, so that `app` walks the path and draws the wavy line without a model.

— *Why:* a task from paper has no module, so no author's question and no fallback text of a module: the opening question is the model's, from the crop; it is kept, as a rung's words are, so that a Hint again costs nothing; and its own answers stand in for the key the leak check has none of before the first Check. Rung 0 of `attempt_rungs` keeps the words beside the rungs' with no new table, and the export carries it as it carries them. The mark follows the setting of the profile, so a learner who chose "questions only" sees no line, and a fallback text, which knows no place, has none.

— *Rejected:* the opening question as part of `GetHint`, which takes no AI and no idempotency key; a column of its own on the attempt for the opening words, which the export and the report would each have to learn; keeping a fallback text, which would hold the attempt to the platform's words after a call that failed once; the line in the mode "questions only", against the learner's choice.

— *Consequence:* `internal/store/migrations/0024_hint_mode.sql`; `internal/store/profiles.go`, `consents.go`, `hints.go`, `view.go`; `internal/account/settings.go`, `profiles.go`, `account.go`; `internal/rpc/methods.go`; `internal/admission/admission.go`; `internal/quota/quota.go`; `internal/debug/debug.go`, `service.go`; `internal/learning/paperhints.go`, `hints.go`, `learning.go`, `report.go`; `internal/tutorstub/tutorstub.go`; `docs/architecture.md`.

## The export of version 4 and what an attempt of paper leaves

**R101. `api` writes version 4 of the export, `exportv4` and `export_format_version` "4": a profile's file holds its hint mode, its open attempts of tasks from paper, `paper_attempts` — each with its rungs, its submissions with their verdicts and observations, and where the key of each task of its crop came from, `key_sources`, never the key —, and an adult's correction of a level by its `correction_id`. An attempt of paper keeps nothing once it closes but its row: its submissions, rungs and count of wrong answers go in the close's transaction, as its key does.**
- **The attempts of paper.** `store.ExportProfile` reads the open attempts of kind `paper` beside those of modules, and their rungs and submissions in the same two queries, `attemptRows`; `export` writes each submission as a module's is, and `key_sources` from the `Key` kept, a task in the order of the crop: `solver` or `ai` by its provenance, `none` with no answer; none before a submission derived the key. `observation.proto` goes in once any submission of either kind is there. A closed attempt of paper is not exported: `api` holds nothing of it but its row.
- **The close** (migration `0025`): `attempts_paper_count_go` sets the count of wrong answers to 0, and `attempts_paper_rows_go` deletes the submissions — and by their cascade the idempotency keys that recorded them — and the rungs, the opening question among them, of an attempt of paper that closes, whichever way; `attempts_paper_key_go` of `0023` erases the key. A problem report on its words stays, as its own row. An attempt of paper closed before the migration loses them in it. The Check that solves the attempt writes no submission, and its key's record keeps the answer with `solved` alone: a repeat of it gets the task solved, which the row's close reason keeps anyway, and no verdict of a task.
- **The correction** of a module's level by an adult is an entry of the profile's log named by its `correction_id`, with no `attempt_id`.

— *Why:* the contract and the platform's rules of privacy: of an attempt of paper `api` keeps the key, the verdicts, the count of wrong answers and the words of the hints only while it is open, and nothing once it ends; its history is the device's, which `app` exports as `paper/`. The row stays for the problem report, which names its attempt; it holds no content.

— *Rejected:* deleting the row of a closed attempt too, which would take the attempt out from under a problem report on its words; keeping the solving submission for its repeat, which would keep its verdicts and what was read past the close; its whole answer in the key's record, the same for two days.

— *Consequence:* `internal/store/export.go`; `internal/store/migrations/0025_paper_close.sql`; `internal/export/export.go`; `internal/learning/paperwork.go`; `docs/architecture.md`.
